# DocScalar Project Brain & Memory Map

This document is the core project memory for DocScalar (formerly DocForge). It outlines the codebase architecture, file responsibilities, recent changes, testing suites, and a complete directory tree. 

---

## 1. Project Overview & Architectural Mission
DocScalar is a high-speed, secure server-side document conversion and visual workflow studio web application.
- **Brand Identity**: **DocScalar** (Document Studio for Secure Archival, Layout & Advanced Rendering). Collisions checked and cleared across GitHub, npm, PyPI, and Docker Hub.
- **Frontend Architecture**: React 18, Vite, React Router v6, and Vanilla CSS. It uses a premium dark glassmorphic design system (`index.css`). All studio interfaces, drag-and-drop dropzones, and interactive previews are paired with server-backed execution and instant guest access (up to 50MB uploads without registration blocks).
- **Backend Architecture**: A FastAPI Python application managing heavy resources (LibreOffice conversion pipelines, PaddleOCR engines, advanced PDF-to-Excel layouts parsing, Ghostscript/pikepdf compressors, and multi-layer auto-bypass PDF unlocking).
- **Communication Flow & Security**: All document uploads are protected via TLS 1.3 encryption in transit, processed asynchronously in memory/transient temporary storage, and automatically purged immediately upon download. The frontend polls job status via [useJobPoller.js](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/hooks/useJobPoller.js) with real-time status messages.
- **UI/UX Design Intelligence**: Style, typography, layouts, and animations are driven by a multi-skill setup integrating the local [ui-ux-pro-max-skill](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/ui-ux-pro-max-skill) folder, `impeccable` rules (banning standard AI slop, enforcing OKLCH contrast, spatial rhythm, and strict anti-patterns), `huashu-design` principles (HTML-native prototypes, slides, animations, and expert reviews), and `taste-skill` presets. Any changes must align with these unified design guardrails.
- **Background Execution (Zero-Polling Protocol)**: Whenever a background task, build process, or long-running command is launched, NEVER poll or check status repeatedly with `manage_task(Action='status')`. Immediately stop calling tools and rest silently until the system delivers the completion notification message.
- **DocScalar OCR Quality Invariant (The 90% / 99% Mandate)**: Under NO circumstances should any OCR model or pipeline be declared deployment-ready for DocScalar unless it reaches:
  1. **Raw Model Benchmark**: $\ge 90\%$ Exact Match Sequence Accuracy generally across real-world document evaluations.
  2. **Enhanced Pipeline (with Language Model / Lexicon / Post-Processing)**: $> 99\%$ Exact Match Accuracy.
  Never settle for preliminary sub-90% checkpoints (e.g. ~43% exact match on `val_lmdb`). Treat them as intermediate stepping stones toward the 90%+ raw and >99% enhanced enterprise standard.

│   │   ├── ocr.py                  # Non-destructive PyMuPDF TextWriter invisible text injection at 250 DPI with 2D Affine Morph Scaling (morph=(Point(px0, baseline_y), Matrix(h_scale, 1.0)) spanning 100% of line width px1 - px0 with subpixel precision, completely eliminating edge-character omissions in Chrome/PDFium previewers), Born-Digital Fast-Path (<2ms bypass for vector pages with CMap/PUA validation and NFKC normalization via validate_cmap_and_mojibake), selective invisible text span redaction (render_mode == 3 / alpha == 0 only, strictly preserving visible render_mode == 0 vector text via purge_invisible_text_spans, clearing fitz_dig_rects), spatial uncovered region detection for sub-page scans/signatures/stamps via detect_uncovered_raster, invisible layer detection via check_has_invisible_layer, natural reading-order sorting (y0 // 10, x0), heal_split_words integration, output_path custom routing, and OS-Level Working Set teardown (EmptyWorkingSet on Windows / malloc_trim on Linux) instantly slashing peak 2.2 GB RAM down to 3.0 MB
│   │   ├── ocr_engine.py           # RobustOCREngine with 2400 max_side high-fidelity resolution, heal_split_words engine (German split compounds erf orderlich->erforderlich, OCR digit repair Messun8->Messung, Datun->Datum, CO2-Ausstoß, scientific decimal point recovery, kerning split-word merging for single-letter and compound fragments, recipe units restoration, and Arabic RTL logical character sequence restoration), Context-Gated 4-Tier Guard (Tier 1 >=90.0% strict optical confidence immunity against any lexicon alteration, Tier 2 PROPER_NAMES & MATH_FUNCTIONS immunity, Tier 3 alphanumeric code immunity, Tier 4 SymSpell dictionary lookup for low-confidence prose preserving casing, punctuation, and contractions with apostrophes), PaddleOCR ONNXRuntime loader with official ONNX models (ru, ar, korean, hi, japan, ch, en), 4x speed optimization, bounded 2-slot reader memory eviction, and auto-teardown with EmptyWorkingSet OS physical memory flush
│   │   ├── pdf_ops.py              # Native C-level PyMuPDF conversion engine (merge, rotate, crop, watermark with 2D morph matrices, dynamic page numbers with subpixel text-width centering, pdf-to-images with per-page pixmap nullification & gc.collect() bounding RAM <25MB, extract pages, remove pages, and image-to-pdf compilation)

## 3. Detailed File Representations (What represents What)

### A. Frontend Pages & View Components (`src/pages/`)
* **[Home.jsx](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/Home.jsx)**: The root router page. It renders the guest landing view overlay (`<LandingPage />`) or routes to the visual workspace canvas view (`/workspace`) if the user is authenticated.
* **[pdf/](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/) page files**: Each page implements a specialized visual workspace:
  * `MergePdf.jsx`: Interactive list to reorder preloaded PDF thumbnails.
  * `SplitPdf.jsx`: Visual page grid with checkboxes and a dual-binding numeric range text field.
  * `CompressPdf.jsx`: Comparison dashboard with original/compressed gauges.
  * `RotatePdf.jsx`: Individually rotates page elements dynamically on hover.
  * `OcrPdf.jsx`: Custom step-by-page reader layout showing page previews sequentially.
  * `UnOcrPdf.jsx`: High-resolution document flattener page stripping selectable text.
  * `SignPdf.jsx`: Left canvas overlay mapping signatures; right panel holding the drawing pad.
  * `EditPdf.jsx`: Professional interactive PDF annotation & direct-edit studio featuring Smart Line Auto-Fit Highlighter with background HPP/digital line analyzer, Direct PDF Whiteout Eraser, In-Situ Direct Text Placement with opaque backdrops, Shape vector tools, High-DPI buffer scaling normalization, and glowing export CTA.
  * `PdfForms.jsx`: Professional interactive PDF Forms Studio & AcroForm Engine supporting form filling, client-side computer vision box detector (`detectCanvasFormBoxes` in `src/utils/pdfFormBoxDetector.js`) for auto-detecting form boxes on flat/scanned PDFs, direct in-place typing into form boxes on the PDF canvas with dynamic font-size scaling and tab navigation, visual field placement (Text, Multiline Textarea, Checkbox, Dropdown, Radio Groups with `onValue` isolation), 8-point bounding transform & move engine, multi-page vertical thumbnail strip, 30-depth undo/redo history stack, arrow key navigation, multi-file session caching, and JSON/CSV data exchange.
  * `ProtectPdf.jsx` / `UnlockPdf.jsx`: SVG dial-combination lock and padlock-key animations matching security states.
* **[image/](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/image/) page files**:
  * `ImageConverter.jsx`: Production-grade Image Converter Studio supporting PNG, JPG, WEBP, BMP, ICO, and PDF formats with pure-JS 24-bit Windows BMP binary encoding, multi-resolution ICO favicon bundling, universal PDF vector fallback embedding, interactive Before/After split comparison slider, keyboard shortcuts (cycling, zoom, reset), natural dimension HUD pills (`naturalDims`), 1-click clipboard image copy, Pro-tier Multi-Format Triple Bundle & 300 DPI print resampling, and lightweight 320px WebP/JPEG thumbnail downsampling for gallery cards with meticulous blob URL revocation on file remove, workspace reset, and unmount.
  * `ImageCompressor.jsx`: Client-side visual image compressor with interactive quality slider, target size estimator, and lightweight 320px thumbnail downsampling for gallery cards while keeping untouched raw `File` instances in state for high-fidelity compression, backed by leak-proof blob URL teardown.
  * `ImageResizer.jsx`: Precision image scaling and aspect ratio locking utility with natural dimension preservation, downsampled 320px thumbnail rendering for batch gallery cards, raw `File` preservation for resizing engines, and leak-proof blob URL revocation.
* **[document/](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/document/) page files**: 
  * `PdfToWord.jsx`: Enterprise PDF to Word studio featuring 2D spatial text clustering, heuristic paragraph reflow & hyphen unwrapping, pre-flight scanned PDF text-density detector (<15 chars/page) with 1-click OCR switch advisory banner, live sidebar telemetry HUD (Document Layer, Est. Words, Total Pages), multi-file batch conversion with ZIP packaging, active file switcher, and omnichannel workflow chaining to Document Viewer, Word to PDF, and Stitch Canvas.

### C. Backend API Server & Core Services (`backend/`)
* **[main.py](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/main.py)**: Initializes the FastAPI app, manages JWT-based login dependencies, registers user account endpoints, enforces file upload limits (blocking files > 200MB), registers GZipMiddleware response compression, and feeds files into the background job worker.
* **[services/ocr.py](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/services/ocr.py)**: Page-by-page OCR engine. It converts PDF pages into high-resolution images, feeds them to `RobustOCREngine`, compiles the characters onto a transparent ReportLab canvas overlay, and serializes a clean, searchable PDF.
* **[services/security.py](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/services/security.py)**: Performs PDF encryption and 4-layer auto-bypass unlocking:
  1. Layer 1: DB hash lookup (retrieves passwords for DocForge-locked files).
  2. Layer 2: Checks empty password (owner-only passwords).
  3. Layer 3: Checks a common dictionary of 500+ words.
  4. Layer 4: Heuristic candidates (generates stems from filenames and date formats).
  Only triggers `PASSWORD_REQUIRED` on frontend client if all layers fail.
* **[services/pdf_to_excel.py](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/services/pdf_to_excel.py)**: Extracts table coordinates and characters from PDF sheets. Word coordinate center-based filtering is applied to map bounding boxes precisely and prevent text bleeding between column edges.
* **[services/libreoffice.py](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/services/libreoffice.py)**: Centralizes headless LibreOffice conversions. Spawns conversion subprocesses safely and routes office conversions (.xlsx, .pptx) to their specialized format layout handlers.
* **[services/digital_sign.py](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/services/digital_sign.py)**: Implements cryptographic X.509 signature sealing using pyHanko, supporting self-signed auto-bypass certificates or uploaded custom PKCS#12 (.p12/.pfx) certificates.
* **[services/url_to_pdf.py](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/services/url_to_pdf.py)**: Renders online websites and links to high-quality print-ready PDFs using headless Playwright Chromium.
* **[services/canvas_agent.py](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/services/canvas_agent.py)**: Spawns plan-parsers that extract structural instructions and operational JSON configurations from complex user command clauses.

### Viewport-Only Virtualized Rendering for Multi-Page PDF Studios (TSK-726 - Topic 6)
* **Goal**: Completely eradicate browser memory exhaustion and UI thread locking when organizing large multi-page documents (e.g. 50-100+ pages) by implementing viewport-only virtualized rendering, zero-canvas skeleton placeholders for offscreen page cards, and aggressive canvas context deallocation on scroll-out.
* **Architecture & Mechanics**:
  - **Viewport Virtualization Utility ([`src/utils/viewportVirtualizer.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/utils/viewportVirtualizer.js))**:
    - `useVisibilityObserver(options = {})`: Cross-browser React hook tracking whether a DOM element is inside the viewport using native `IntersectionObserver` with a configurable prefetch buffer (`rootMargin = '200px'`, `threshold = 0.01`). Safe fallback defaulting `isVisible = true` when IntersectionObserver is not supported (SSR, headless testing environments, legacy browsers) ensuring no content is ever blocked. Returns `[ref, isVisible]` with property aliases `.ref` and `.isVisible`.
    - `createVirtualWindow(totalItems, activeIndex = 0, windowSize = 3)`: Mathematical bounded window calculator returning `[start, end]` index array with attached metadata (`.start`, `.end`, `.count`, `.indices`, `.isInWindow(idx)`).
    - `computeVirtualizationSavings(totalItems, viewportItems, bytesPerPage)`: Computes exact memory reduction formula `Math.round(((saved) / total) * 10000) / 100`. For 100 pages (14.46MB unvirtualized vs 867.8KB virtualized), saves 13.6MB (94.00% RAM reduction, exceeding the 90% threshold).
  - **Organize Studio Virtualization ([`src/components/FocusStudio/studios/OrganizeStudio.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/FocusStudio/studios/OrganizeStudio.jsx))**:
    - In `PageCard`, integrated `useVisibilityObserver({ rootMargin: '200px', threshold: 0.01 })`.
    - When offscreen (`!isVisible`), PageCard renders a zero-canvas skeleton placeholder (`data-testid="page-skeleton"`) preserving exact `aspect-[3/4]` card geometry, min-height (`min-h-[140px]`), and accessible aria-label without allocating any `<canvas>` DOM node or VRAM backing store.
    - When scrolling into the viewport, instantiates the canvas and renders the DPR-capped thumbnail.
    - On scroll-out, `PageCard` cleanup immediately invokes `safeCancelRenderTask(renderTask)` and `releaseCanvas(canvasRef.current)`, collapsing canvas width and height to 0 and reclaiming backing memory.
  - **Compare Studio Eviction ([`src/components/FocusStudio/studios/CompareStudio.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/FocusStudio/studios/CompareStudio.jsx))**:
    - Added deterministic canvas eviction across page flips and viewMode transitions, releasing all 4 canvas targets (`canvasARef.current`, `canvasBRef.current`, `canvasAOverlayRef.current`, `canvasBOverlayRef.current`) via `releaseCanvas` and canceling in-flight render tasks via `safeCancelRenderTask`.
* **Molecular Verification**: Authored `tests/frontend/test_viewport_virtualization_suite.mjs` verifying all 118 assertions across 36 distinct test areas with 100% pass rate. Certified clean production build (`npm run build`) in 51.96s with exit code 0.

### Image Studio Preview Downsampling & Memory Guard (TSK-725 - Topic 5)
* **Goal**: Prevent browser memory exhaustion and multi-gigabyte RAM spikes when batch-compressing, converting, or resizing dozens of high-resolution DSLR photos (e.g. 4000x3000, 20MB each). Downsample gallery preview thumbnails offscreen to max 320px WebP/JPEG images while preserving untouched raw `File` instances in state for high-fidelity conversion engines, backed by leak-proof blob URL teardown.
* **Architecture & Mechanics**:
  - **Image Preview Helper ([`src/utils/imagePreviewHelper.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/utils/imagePreviewHelper.js))**:
    - `computeDownscaledDimensions(width, height, maxDimension = 320)`: Proportional aspect ratio downscaling, capping longest edge at `maxDimension` with 1px floor clamp for extreme ribbons and resilient to negative/NaN dimensions.
    - `computeBitmapMemoryBytes(width, height)`: Calculates backing pixel buffer memory formula `w * h * 4` bytes.
    - `generateImageThumbnail(file, options = { maxDimension: 320, quality: 0.8 })`: Offscreen canvas rendering, lightweight WebP export with JPEG fallback per WHATWG canvas spec, guaranteed temporary canvas collapse via `releaseCanvas(canvas)` in a `finally` block, returning `{ previewUrl, width, height, originalWidth, originalHeight, revoke: () => URL.revokeObjectURL(previewUrl) }`.
    - `generateBatchImageThumbnails(files, options)`: Batch worker pool with cancellation token (`cancellationToken.isCancelled` / `signal.aborted`) support, `Promise.allSettled` worker synchronization guaranteeing all in-flight workers settle and register created blobs before cleanup, and immediate revocation of all allocated thumbnails on cancellation or error via `safeRevokeUrls(allocatedThumbnails)`, eliminating orphaned blob URL leaks.
    - `safeRevokeUrls(urls)`: Idempotent, exception-shielded revocation helper handling blob URL strings, thumbnail objects, arrays, Sets, Maps, dictionaries/records, and protected against circular references via `WeakSet`.
  - **Studio Integrations**:

Preserved `naturalDims` dimension metadata and synchronized state filtering on `handleRemoveFile` in `ImageConverter`.

### DPR-Aware Thumbnail Resolution Capping & Offscreen Rendering (TSK-724 - Topic 4)
* **Goal**: Prevent massive GPU/V8 memory bloat from ultra-large documents (e.g. 3000pt+ architectural blueprints consuming 30.7MB+ per thumbnail) and ultra-high pixel ratio displays (3x/4x mobile/retina devices). Enforce DPR-aware viewport resolution capping, dynamic scale bounding [0.05, 2.0], offscreen rendering to lightweight image data URLs, and 100% leak-proof canvas memory teardown.
* **Architecture & Mechanics**:
  - **DPR-Aware Thumbnail Helper ([`src/utils/thumbnailHelper.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/utils/thumbnailHelper.js))**:
    - `computeThumbnailViewport(page, options = {})`: Options default to `targetWidth = 160`, `maxDpr = 2`, `rotation = 0`. Computes dynamic scale: `(targetWidth * Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, maxDpr)) / unscaledWidth`. Clamps scale strictly between `0.05` (guards 3000pt+ blueprints) and `2.0` (guards microscopic PDFs). Guards against corrupt page dimensions (`NaN`, `Infinity`, or `<= 0`) with `isFinite` validation. Preserves page rotation (0°, 90°, 180°, 270°) and inherent `page.rotate` fallback. Returns bounded viewport via `page.getViewport({ scale, rotation })`.
    - `computeThumbnailMemoryBytes(width, height)`: Calculates backing pixel buffer memory using the exact formula `Math.round(width) * Math.round(height) * 4` bytes.
    - `renderThumbnailToCanvas(page, canvas, options = {})`: Sizes target canvas (handling HTMLCanvasElement and React refs `{ current: canvas }`), clears existing pixels via `clearRect`, cancels any prior active render task via `safeCancelRenderTask`, tracks active task on `canvas._docscalarRenderTask`, forwards `intent`, `transform`, and render options, chains `.catch(() => {})` after `.finally()` to eradicate unhandled promise rejections on render cancellation, and returns `{ renderTask, viewport, width, height }`.
    - `renderThumbnailToDataUrl(page, options = {})`: Renders offscreen and converts to lightweight image data URL (JPEG/WebP, quality 0.8), defaults to opaque white `#FFFFFF` page background for all raster formats, and wraps execution in `try ... finally` to guarantee the temporary canvas is immediately freed via `releaseCanvas(canvas)`.
  - **Organize Studio Integration ([`src/components/FocusStudio/studios/OrganizeStudio.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/FocusStudio/studios/OrganizeStudio.jsx))**:
    - In `PageCard`, eradicated legacy hardcoded `scale: 0.8` and integrated `computeThumbnailViewport(page, { targetWidth: 160, maxDpr: 2 })`, slashing blueprint thumbnail memory from 30.7MB to 136KB (>99.5% RAM savings) while strictly bounding thumbnail memory under 150KB.
  - **Merge PDF Studio Integration ([`src/pages/pdf/MergePdf.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/MergePdf.jsx))**:
    - Defined and exported `generateThumbnail(firstPage, file, options = {})` and `getPagePreview` alias. Replaced legacy hardcoded `scale: 0.35` in the file loading loop with `computeThumbnailViewport(firstPage, { targetWidth: 160, maxDpr: 2 })`. Wrapped canvas and cropCanvas generation in nested `try ... finally` blocks guaranteeing both are freed via `releaseCanvas(canvas)` and `releaseCanvas(cropCanvas)` with SSR/headless environment guards.
* **Molecular Verification**: Authored `tests/frontend/test_thumbnail_dpr_cap_suite.mjs` verifying all 103 assertions across 46 distinct test areas with 100% pass rate. Certified clean production build (`npm run build`) with exit code 0.

### Non-Blocking State Persistence via `requestIdleCallback` (TSK-722 - Topic 3)
* **Goal**: Eliminate UI thread frame drops, drag stutter, and input latency caused by synchronous localStorage serialization and IndexedDB state persistence across DocScalar. Provide cooperative background scheduling adhering to the W3C Cooperative Scheduling of Background Tasks specification with universal cross-browser fallback.
* **Architecture & Mechanics**:
  - **Cooperative Idle Task Scheduler ([`src/utils/idleTask.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/utils/idleTask.js))**:
    - `scheduleIdleTask(callback, options = { timeout: 2000 })`: Delegates directly to `window.requestIdleCallback` when available with `{ timeout }` forwarding. For Safari, Web Workers, Node.js, and legacy runtimes lacking native support, schedules cooperative macrotasks via `setTimeout` passing a mock `IdleDeadline` strictly conforming to W3C specs: `timeRemaining()` calculating remaining milliseconds in a 50ms idle slice with monotonic floor at 0ms and returning exactly 0 if `didTimeout === true` (per W3C Section 4.1.2), and `didTimeout` calculating whether elapsed time exceeds requested timeout. Catches unhandled rejections from async callbacks in both native and fallback modes.
    - `cancelIdleTask(id)`: Safely cancels tasks in both native and fallback modes with active task registry eviction and raw timer handle clearing.
    - `runWhenIdle(asyncFn, timeoutMs = 2000)`: Promise helper that executes an async function during idle time and resolves with its return value, propagates errors, and exposes a `.cancel(reason)` method.
    - `getActiveIdleTaskCount()` and `clearAllIdleTasks()`: Active task tracking for diagnostics, memory leak validation (snapshotting keys before iteration), and clean teardowns.
  - **Non-Blocking Persistence in Local IndexedDB Utility ([`src/utils/db.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/utils/db.js))**:
    - `saveFile()`: Defer `localStorage.setItem('conversionHistory', ...)` serialization and I/O using `scheduleIdleTask` with 2000ms timeout so heavy JSON stringification never blocks UI animation frames while immediately resolving the saved file ID. Auto-heals corrupted JSON in localStorage with fallback to clean arrays and auto-trims to <=25 items on QuotaExceededError.
    - `saveCanvasState(state)` / `clearCanvasState()`: Wrapped IndexedDB state persistence in `runWhenIdle(..., 2000)` with sequence counters (`canvasSaveSequence`) and in-flight cancellation, eliminating write races and completely preventing resurrection of cleared canvas state.
    - `saveStudioDraft(key, data)` / `clearStudioDraft(key)`: Wrapped draft persistence and QuotaExceededError retry recovery in `runWhenIdle(..., 2000)` with per-key debouncing (`pendingDraftSaveTasks`) and sequence checking, eliminating typing write storms and preventing draft resurrection on clear.
    - `pruneExpiredStudioDrafts(maxAgeMs)`: Scheduled background sweeps in `runWhenIdle(..., 3000)` with shared Promise coalescing across concurrent calls.
* **Molecular Verification**: Authored `tests/frontend/test_idle_task_persistence_suite.mjs` verifying all 90 assertions across 38 distinct test areas (100% pass rate). Certified clean production build (`npm run build`) in 1m 53s with exit code 0.

### Phase 3: Canvas Virtual Bundle & Intermediate Background Pipeline Optimization (TSK-717)
* **Goal**: Remediate canvas card sprawl and excessive RAM consumption when multi-page tools (e.g. `pdf-to-image` on a 43-page PDF, `split-pdf`, `unzip-file`) run in pipelines or single execution. Prevent dozens of physical DOM cards and cables from clogging the workspace while preserving 100% of individual files in memory for downstream operators and in downloadable result archives.
* **Architecture & Mechanics**:
  - **Threshold Constant**: Exported `MAX_INDIVIDUAL_CANVAS_CARDS = 5` in `src/utils/canvasBrain.js` and `src/components/StitchCanvas.jsx`.
  - **Pure Decision Logic**: Implemented `shouldUnpackCanvasCards(hasDownstreamConsumers, totalFiles, maxCards = 5)` and `checkHasDownstreamConsumers(opId, ordered, operations)`.
  - **Intermediate Pipeline Output Suppression**: In `runPipeline` (lines 5497-5545), when an operator has downstream consumers (`ordered.some(...) || operations.some(...)`), it completely bypasses unpacking multiple cards on the canvas. Instead, it preserves the complete list of files in `pipelineOutputs[op.id] = { outputs: outputsList, ... }` and creates at most 1 representative consolidated bundle card (`.zip`). Downstream operators consume all files in-memory directly via `for (const sub of out.outputs)`.
  - **Large Batch Output Cap (> 5 files)**: For operations producing > 5 files (e.g. 10, 43, 100 images), suppresses multiple card spawning and creates exactly 1 consolidated collection card (e.g. `${baseName}_images.zip` or `${baseName}_outputs.zip`) while keeping all sub-files in memory and in the downloadable zip blob.
  - **Small Batch Unpack Parity (<= 5 files)**: Preserves clean 1-to-1 card unrolling for terminal operators generating small batches (<= 5 files) without downstream consumers.
  - **Dual-Indexed Memory Mapping**: Mapped `pipelineOutputs[outputNodeId] = pipelineOutputs[op.id]` ensuring downstream consumers connected via either operator ID or collection output ID resolve all in-memory files instantly.
  - **Molecular Verification**: Authored `tests/frontend/test_canvas_card_sprawl_and_pipeline_suite.mjs` verifying all 40 assertions (100% pass rate). Certified clean production build (`npm run build`).

### DocScalar Multi-Tier Smart Cropper Quota Architecture, Zero Auto-Crop Guest/Free Suppression, Daily 3-Click Entitlement Enforcement, Modal Gating, and Multi-Studio Remediation (TSK-626)
* **Goal**: Completely eradicate unauthorized auto-cropping in guest mode, suppress automatic crop execution across all PDF studios on file upload, enforce a strict multi-tier entitlement model (0 access for guests, exactly 3 free crops per calendar day for registered users, unlimited for Pro subscribers), provide informative gating modals with signup/login/upgrade CTAs, and ensure zero visual regressions.
* **Implementation Details**:
  - In [`src/utils/smartCropQuota.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/utils/smartCropQuota.js):
    - Multi-Tier Quota Manager: Established `DAILY_FREE_LIMIT = 3`, `getTodayDateKey()` for local calendar day resolution, and `getQuotaStorageKey(userEmail, dateKey)`.
    - Entitlement Evaluation: Implemented `getSmartCropQuota(auth)`, returning `{ allowed, reason, used, remaining, limit, isPro, isAuthenticated, date }`. Guests get `allowed: false, reason: 'guest_locked'`. Free users get 3 clicks per calendar day (`Math.max(0, 3 - used)`). Pro subscribers get `allowed: true, reason: 'pro_unlimited', remaining: Infinity`.
    - Click Consumption & Deductions: Implemented `consumeSmartCropClick(auth)`, deducting 1 click for free users up to 3 and emitting `docscalar_smart_crop_quota_changed` on the window for reactive UI synchronization. Pro users consume 0 clicks.
  - In [`src/components/SmartCropLimitModal.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/SmartCropLimitModal.jsx):
    - Glassmorphic Dialog: Created React Portal modal with keyboard ESC dismissal, backdrop click dismissal, and accessible ARIA attributes.
    - Guest Mode Variant (`type="guest"`): Renders "Unlock Smart Cropper", educational copy on border detection, "Sign Up Free (3 Crops Daily)" CTA directing to `/signup`, and "Log In" link directing to `/login`.
    - Daily Limit Reached Variant (`type="limit_reached"`): Renders "Daily Limit Reached (3/3 Used)", midnight reset information, and "Upgrade to Pro for Unlimited Crops" CTA directing to `/pricing`.
  - In [`src/hooks/useSmartCropper.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/hooks/useSmartCropper.js):
    - Strict Auto-Apply Suppression: `isActive` state defaults to `false` for all guests and free users. Auto-apply is restricted strictly to `isProUser && shouldAutoApplySmartCrop()`.
    - Gate on `activate(pdfBytes)`: Checks `getSmartCropQuota(auth)`. If unauthenticated or limit reached, sets `limitModalType`, opens `limitModalOpen`, and returns `false`. If free user, consumes 1 click via `consumeSmartCropClick(auth)`.
    - Non-Deducting Deactivation: `deactivate()` / `revert()` restores the uncropped document without consuming clicks.
  - In [`src/components/SmartCropperToolbarButton.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/SmartCropperToolbarButton.jsx) & [`src/components/SmartCropperPanel.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/SmartCropperPanel.jsx):
    - Dynamic Tier Badges: Displays `PRO` badge for Pro subscribers, `(X/3 left)` for authenticated free users, and `[lock] Free 3/day` for guests.
    - Integrated Gating Modal: Automatically mounts `<SmartCropLimitModal>` listening to hook or local modal states.
  - In Studio Pages (`OrganizePdf.jsx`, `ExtractPages.jsx`, `RemovePages.jsx`, `RotatePdf.jsx`, `SplitPdf.jsx`):
    - Removed unconditional `cropper.activate()` calls from file upload parsing loops.
    - Updated thumbnail canvas painting to respect `cropper.isActive` rather than `cropper.isActive || hasScanBorders`.
* **Quantitative Verification**:

### DocScalar Complete Server-Side Native Engine Migration, Browser RAM Spike Elimination & IP Protection (TSK-719)
* **Goal**: Remediate client-side code exposure, eliminate browser memory crashes (specifically the 2.2 GB V8 RAM explosion caused by in-browser canvas rendering during PDF to Image / heavy ops), protect proprietary algorithms from code theft, eradicate misleading "in-browser / local processing" marketing claims, and enforce server-first execution across DocScalar with resilient client fallbacks.

    - Watermark Morph Engine: Used 2D affine morph matrix (`morph=(center_point, fitz.Matrix(rotation))`) for arbitrary watermark rotation angles, eliminating PyMuPDF 90-degree integer step limitations.
    - Page Numbers Engine: Subpixel string width calculation using `fitz.get_text_length(text, fontsize)` across 6 coordinate anchors (`bottom-center`, `bottom-right`, `bottom-left`, `top-center`, `top-right`, `top-left`) with dynamic page numbering format templates (`{page} of {total}`).

    - Added 9 dedicated async endpoints (`/api/convert/merge-pdf`, `/api/convert/rotate-pdf`, `/api/convert/crop-pdf`, `/api/convert/add-watermark`, `/api/convert/add-page-numbers`, `/api/convert/pdf-to-image`, `/api/convert/extract-pages`, `/api/convert/remove-pages`, `/api/convert/image-to-pdf`) supporting file IDs, file lists, and direct uploads.

### DocForge OCR Engine 4x Speed Optimization & Bounded RAM Cache Eviction (TSK-600)
* **Goal**: Reduce full-page CPU detection inference time by 4x, cap PaddleOCR memory footprint across multi-language processing, and optimize candidate probing to use line crops without unconstrained full-page inference.
* **Implementation Details**:
  - In [`backend/services/ocr_engine.py`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/services/ocr_engine.py):
    - Added `text_det_limit_side_len: 960` and `text_det_limit_type: "max"` to `ocr_kwargs` and `fallback_kwargs` in `get_paddleocr_reader`, dropping detection time from 14.86s to 4.78s (3.9x speedup) with 100% text recovery.
    - Implemented bounded 2-slot reader eviction in `get_paddleocr_reader` popping the oldest cached reader and invoking `gc.collect()` whenever `len(_PADDLEOCR_READERS) >= 2`.
    - Optimized candidate script probing in `_phase3_extract`: probes at most the first 2 line crops using `cand_reader.paddlex_pipeline.predict(crop)` only if `target_script is None` and boxes exist, and completely avoids full-page candidate prediction if `header_text` already confirmed a script.

### DocForge High Precision OCR Sub-Pixel Coordinate Alignment (TSK-594)
* **Goal**: Eliminate the ~44pt vertical coordinate displacement that pushed OCR text bounding boxes into the blank top page margin (causing solid blue selection blocks over whitespace in Google Chrome and Microsoft Edge).
* **Implementation Details**:
  - In [`backend/services/ocr_engine.py`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/services/ocr_engine.py):
    - Root Cause: In PaddleX v3, `use_doc_unwarping` defaults to `True`, triggering the `UVDoc_onnx` document unwarping model. UVDoc crops off page margins to rectify physical curvature, returning detection polygons in a cropped coordinate space where $y=0$ is shifted into the visual text area. When scaled back by `page_h / pix_h`, this shifted every text line upward by ~44 PDF points into the top page margin.
    - Fix: Explicitly set `use_doc_unwarping: False` and `use_doc_orientation_classify: False` in both `ocr_kwargs` and `fallback_kwargs` in `get_paddleocr_reader`. This preserves strict 1:1 affine correspondence with the uncropped original page pixmap.
  - In [`backend/tests/test_ocr_auto_teardown_and_bounds.py`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/tests/test_ocr_auto_teardown_and_bounds.py):
    - Expanded test suite from 15 to 32 falsifiable molecular test assertions covering unwarping invariants, coordinate scaling, digital exclusion, sub-pixel baseline math, dynamic font shrinking, and memory teardown (32/32 passing 100%).
  - Empirical Verification on 13-Page Real Handwritten Document (`ocr_test_handwritten.pdf`):
    - Generated `backend/output/ocr_test_handwritten_ocr.pdf`. Verified coordinates across all pages: Page 0 header at $y=[13.77, 28.98]\text{pt}$, title `English` at $y=[45.46, 62.89]\text{pt}$, `Personal` at $y=[45.46, 62.89]\text{pt}$, `Letter` at $y=[45.46, 62.89]\text{pt}$, `Dear` at $y=[76.41, 93.10]\text{pt}$; Page 1 `French` at $y=[45.47, 65.13]\text{pt}$; Page 2 `Spanish` at $y=[45.47, 65.13]\text{pt}$; Page 3 `German` at $y=[45.47, 64.38]\text{pt}$; Page 4 `Russian` at $y=[45.47, 65.13]\text{pt}$.
    - Word selections in Chrome and Acrobat align with sub-pixel exactness over the visual ink.

### DocForge OCR Engine Auto-Teardown Memory Reclamation & Multilingual Routing (TSK-592)
* **Goal**: Eradicate 1.65GB RAM process linger after OCR execution, fix 5% freeze in High Precision server OCR, eliminate Chrome full-page solid blue selection block, and support auto-detect all languages + strict English filtering.
* **Implementation Details**:
  - In [`backend/services/ocr_engine.py`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/services/ocr_engine.py):
    - Engineered `clear_paddleocr_readers()` to forcefully evict all cached PaddleOCR/PaddleX predictor instances (`_pipeline`, `predictor`, `paddlex_pipeline`, `page_ocr`, `text_detector`, `text_recognizer`, `text_classifier`), flush CUDA cache via `paddle.device.cuda.empty_cache()`, and run `gc.collect()`.
    - Added `"all"`, `"auto"`, `"multilingual"`, and `"chi_sim"` aliases to `PADDLE_LANG_MAPPING` routing to `"ch"`.
    - Set default `engine: "onnxruntime"` in `get_paddleocr_reader` to bypass Windows oneDNN fatal PIR attribute crash (`ConvertPirAttribute2RuntimeAttribute not support [pir::ArrayAttribute<pir::DoubleAttribute>]`).
    - Restored official `PP-OCRv6_medium_rec_onnx` (98.5% confidence on handwriting), eliminating empty string recognition and destructive fallback to Tesseract.
  - In [`backend/services/ocr.py`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/services/ocr.py):
    - Implemented `teardown_ocr_engine()` and invoked it inside `run_ocr_on_pdf`'s `finally:` block, guaranteeing memory is returned immediately to Windows OS (~210MB residual).
    - Replaced hard 5.0pt font floor with dynamic scaling down to 1.0pt (`max(1.0, font_size * (box_w / t_len))`), preventing text spilling outside bounding boxes and eliminating Chrome full-page electric blue selection blocks.
    - Added granular progress tracking (`10 + int((completed / total) * 78)`), eliminating 5% progress freeze.

### Repair PDF & Redact PDF Production-Grade Remediation (TSK-572)
* **Goal**: Elevate the Repair PDF and Redact PDF tools (and required shared components) to an enterprise production-grade standard: real 4-tier structural recovery, true vector & pixel permanent redaction, exact subpixel character token bounding, studio ergonomics (step zoom, touch swipe, keyboard arrow navigation), and zero engine disclosures or AI marketing slop.
* **Implementation Details**:
  - In [`backend/services/repair.py`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/services/repair.py):
    - Implemented 4-stage recovery pipeline: Stage 1 pikepdf XREF repair & object stream rebuilding; Stage 2 PyMuPDF deep garbage collection (`garbage=4, deflate=True`); Stage 3 Page salvage preserving valid pages from corrupted streams; Stage 4 High-DPI raster fallback synthesizing fresh PDF pages when vector streams are destroyed.
    - Added `@app.post("/api/repair-pdf")`, `@app.post("/api/convert/repair-pdf")`, `@app.get("/health")`, and `@app.get("/api/health")` in [`backend/main.py`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/main.py).
    - Verified with 6/6 tests passing in [`backend/tests/test_repair_pdf.py`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/tests/test_repair_pdf.py).
  - In [`src/engines/pdf/repairPdf.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/pdf/repairPdf.js):
    - Engineered 3-tier client recovery (pdf-lib clean reload -> backend service -> PDF.js high-DPI rasterization).
    - Supported polymorphic return signature: pure `Blob` when `{ returnBlobOnly: true }` and metadata object `{ success, blob, pageCount, size, strategy }` by default.
  - In [`src/components/StitchCanvas.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/StitchCanvas.jsx):
    - Lines 2604 & 3642: Passed `{ returnBlobOnly: true }` and unwrapped `repairRes instanceof Blob ? repairRes : (repairRes.blob || repairRes)`, fixing DAG canvas execution.
  - In [`src/engines/pdf/redactPdf.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/pdf/redactPdf.js):
    - True permanent redaction: Routes to backend PyMuPDF vector purge; falls back to client-side high-DPI canvas rasterization on redacted pages (`renderScale = 2.25`) with opaque black fill and 1.5px subpixel bleed, permanently destroying underlying text bytes while preserving non-redacted pages as 100% vector.
    - Sanitizes document metadata (Title, Author, Subject, Keywords).
  - In [`src/utils/piiDetector.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/utils/piiDetector.js):
    - Replaced whole-line blackouts with exact subpixel character token bounds (`subTx = item.x + (matchStart / itemText.length) * item.width`, `subWidth = (matchLen / itemText.length) * item.width`).
    - Fixed phone masking regex to preserve whitespace, parentheses, and hyphens while masking digits.
  - In [`src/components/FocusStudio/studios/RedactStudio.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/FocusStudio/studios/RedactStudio.jsx):
    - Fixed coordinate scaling skew via `scaleX = overlay.width / rect.width` and `scaleY = overlay.height / rect.height`.
    - Added touch drag and tap listeners for mobile/tablet ergonomics.
  - In [`src/pages/pdf/RepairPdf.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/RepairPdf.jsx) & [`src/pages/pdf/RedactPdf.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/RedactPdf.jsx):
    - Standardized single-row step zoom widget `[-] 100% [+]` with non-collapsible `shrink-0` bounds.
    - Added keyboard arrow navigation (`ArrowLeft`/`ArrowRight`), touch swipe physics, and Escape key handling.
    - Purged internal engine badges ("XREF Engine Ready", "Ghostscript", "PyMuPDF") and marketing slop ("cryptographic pixel purging", "AI-Powered").
  - In [`src/components/AIChatBot.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/AIChatBot.jsx):
    - Added `/repair-pdf` to studio paths, preventing floating Copilot button from occluding CTAs.
* **Target Files**: [`src/engines/pdf/repairPdf.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/pdf/repairPdf.js), [`src/engines/pdf/redactPdf.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/pdf/redactPdf.js), [`src/utils/piiDetector.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/utils/piiDetector.js), [`src/pages/pdf/RepairPdf.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/RepairPdf.jsx), [`src/pages/pdf/RedactPdf.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/RedactPdf.jsx), [`src/components/FocusStudio/studios/RedactStudio.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/FocusStudio/studios/RedactStudio.jsx), [`src/components/StitchCanvas.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/StitchCanvas.jsx), [`backend/services/repair.py`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/services/repair.py), [`backend/main.py`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/main.py), [`backend/tests/test_repair_pdf.py`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/tests/test_repair_pdf.py), [`tests/frontend/test_repair_and_redact_pdf_suite.mjs`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/tests/frontend/test_repair_and_redact_pdf_suite.mjs).

### PDF Forms Studio & AcroForm Engine Enterprise Hardening (TSK-567 - TSK-570)
* **Goal**: Transform the PDF Forms Studio (`/pdf-forms`) into an enterprise production-grade interactive AcroForm creator and filler, addressing radio group collisions, arrow key freezes, missing multiline textareas, 8-point bounding transform manipulation, toolbar layout integrity, and guest accessibility.
* **Implementation Details**:
  - In [`src/engines/pdf/pdfForms.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/pdf/pdfForms.js):
    - Extracted widget `onValue` in `getFormFields` via `widget.getOnValue?.()`, `acroField.getExportValues()`, `getOnValues()`, and fallback options array for radio groups.
    - Added multiline detection in `compileFormWithNewFields` via `fieldDef.multiline || pdfHeight > 35`.
    - Added resilient name collision guards (`try { form.get... } catch { form.create... }`) for text fields, checkboxes, and dropdowns.
    - Added radio option fallback (`fieldDef.value || 'Option_' + (pageIndex + 1)`).
  - In [`src/pages/pdf/PdfForms.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/PdfForms.jsx):
    - Radio Group Option Isolation: Passed `onValue` to existing canvas widgets and wired values, checked state, and change handlers per option value.
    - 8-Point Resize & Move Transform Engine: Added `RESIZE_HANDLES` (`nw`, `n`, `ne`, `e`, `se`, `s`, `sw`, `w`), center move handle, subpixel bounds clamping (0–100%), minimum 2% dimensions, and 2.5% grid snapping (`snapToGrid`).
    - Undo/Redo History Stack: Added `undoStack` and `redoStack` states (30-depth cap) with toolbar buttons, `Ctrl/Cmd+Z` and `Ctrl/Cmd+Y` shortcuts.
    - Window Keyboard Navigation: Page navigation (`ArrowLeft`/`PageUp`, `ArrowRight`/`PageDown`), field deletion (`Delete`/`Backspace`), field nudging (`Arrow` keys with 0.5% / 2.0% Shift steps), and deselect (`Escape`).
    - Vertical Page Thumbnail Strip: Added left-side thumbnail navigation strip (`w-28`) with real-time field count badges and active page highlighting.
    - Multi-File Session State Isolation: Engineered `fileSessionsRef` cache and `switchFile` function preventing data bleeding across multi-file pools.
    - Non-Wrapping Toolbar & Precision Step Zoom: Refactored toolbar to `shrink-0 min-h-[48px]`, capped filename width to `max-w-[130px] truncate`, and replaced squashed 64px range slider with clean step zoom widget (`[-] 100% [+]`).
  - In [`src/components/AIChatBot.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/AIChatBot.jsx):
    - Added `location.pathname.startsWith('/pdf-forms')` to `isStudio`, ensuring floating Copilot button docks safely left of the sidebar, eliminating occlusion over the Save CTA.
  - In [`src/utils/constants.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/utils/constants.js):
    - Updated `pdf-forms` `minTier` from `'free'` to `'guest'`, enabling unauthenticated visitors to use the 100% client-side studio without being blocked.
* **Target Files**: [`src/engines/pdf/pdfForms.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/pdf/pdfForms.js), [`src/pages/pdf/PdfForms.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/PdfForms.jsx), [`src/components/FocusStudio/studios/FormFillStudio.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/FocusStudio/studios/FormFillStudio.jsx), [`src/components/AIChatBot.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/AIChatBot.jsx), [`src/utils/constants.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/utils/constants.js), [`tests/frontend/test_pdf_forms_studio_suite.mjs`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/tests/frontend/test_pdf_forms_studio_suite.mjs), [`tests/frontend/test_pdf_forms_adversarial_suite.mjs`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/tests/frontend/test_pdf_forms_adversarial_suite.mjs).

* **Goal**: Provide 100% visual and export parity for Universal Smart Cropping across multi-page gallery tools (`SplitPdf`, `RotatePdf`, `OrganizePdf`, `ExtractPages`, `RemovePages`), eliminating uncropped thumbnail scan borders and multi-page export bottlenecks.
* **Implementation Details**:

  - In [`src/pages/pdf/SplitPdf.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/SplitPdf.jsx), [`src/pages/pdf/RotatePdf.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/RotatePdf.jsx), and [`src/pages/pdf/OrganizePdf.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/OrganizePdf.jsx):
    - Implemented `rawCanvasCacheRef` and `detectionCacheRef` to cache uncropped offscreen canvas buffers.

    - Added emerald ring styling (`ring-2 ring-emerald-500/50 bg-emerald-500/5`) to actively cropped page cards.
  - In [`src/pages/pdf/ExtractPages.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/ExtractPages.jsx) and [`src/pages/pdf/RemovePages.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/RemovePages.jsx):
    - Precomputed `croppedDataUrl` using an offscreen canvas with `forceActive: true` in `loadThumbnails`.
    - Swapped `src` between `thumb.croppedDataUrl` and `thumb.dataUrl` reactively based on `cropper.isActive && thumb.hasScanBorders`.
  - In [`src/workers/pdf.worker.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/workers/pdf.worker.js):
    - Replaced single-page `{ 0: docCropBox }` with multi-page `targetCropBoxes = options.pageCropBoxes || options.docCropBoxes || ...` in `handleSplitPdf` and `handleMergePdf`.
    - Guarded backend dispatch in `SplitPdf.jsx` (`backendActive && !cropper.isActive`), routing cropped splits through the worker engine.
  - In [`src/engines/pdf/organizePdf.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/pdf/organizePdf.js), [`src/engines/pdf/extractPages.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/pdf/extractPages.js), and [`src/engines/pdf/removePages.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/pdf/removePages.js):

### A. Desktop App Black/White Screen Flash Resolution & Relative Asset Pathing (TSK-250)
* **Goal**: Eliminate black and white window flashes on desktop app startup, resolve dynamic chunk loading 404s, and prevent infinite ErrorBoundary reload loops.
* **Implementation Details**:
  - Configured `base: './'` in [`vite.config.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/vite.config.js), forcing all lazy-loaded React chunks, CSS, workers, and fonts to resolve relatively to `index.html` across Web and WebView2 environments.
  - Added `"visible": false` and `"backgroundColor": "#090b0f"` in [`tauri.conf.json`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json) to match the dark theme tokens natively on OS window creation.
  - Added a mount `useEffect` in [`App.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/App.jsx) invoking `getCurrentWebviewWindow().show()`, revealing the desktop window seamlessly once React has rendered.
  - Updated [`ErrorBoundary.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/ErrorBoundary.jsx) to disable automatic `window.location.reload()` calls when running inside Tauri desktop mode.
  - Updated [`main.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/main.jsx) `displayCrashScreen` to filter out non-fatal background network/UI warnings.
* **Target Files**: [`vite.config.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/vite.config.js), [`src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json), [`src/App.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/App.jsx), [`src/components/ErrorBoundary.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/ErrorBoundary.jsx), [`src/main.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/main.jsx).

### B. DocForge Live Repo Map Watcher System (TSK-239-Old)
* **Goal**: Build a continuous, zero-token-cost background codebase indexing system (`scripts/watch_repo.js` + `scripts/parse_python.py`) that monitors all 25k+ lines of source code in real time, auto-updating a precise `repo_map.md` symbol map whenever any file is edited and saved.
* **Implementation Details**:
  - Built `scripts/watch_repo.js` leveraging `@babel/parser`, `@babel/traverse`, and `chokidar` (v3.6.0) with Windows atomic save stability (`awaitWriteFinish: { stabilityThreshold: 150 }`) and safe git history queries (`--no-optional-locks`).
  - Built `scripts/parse_python.py` using Python's AST parser to extract Python functions, classes, arguments, docstrings, and call chains in ~50ms per file.
  - Automatically updates `repo_map.md` (<300ms latency) containing file-by-file symbol tables (line ranges, arguments, key call targets) and recent commit history.
  - Configured `run_project.bat` to launch `npm run watch-repo` in the background alongside Vite and FastAPI servers.
  - Enforced Token Efficiency Protocol in `.agents/AGENTS.md` requiring AI sessions to read `repo_map.md` first and use line-ranged reads (`view_file(StartLine, EndLine)`), cutting token usage by ~92%.
* **Target Files**: [`scripts/watch_repo.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/scripts/watch_repo.js), [`scripts/parse_python.py`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/scripts/parse_python.py), [`package.json`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/package.json), [`run_project.bat`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/run_project.bat), [`vite.config.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/vite.config.js), [`.gitignore`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/.gitignore), [`.agents/AGENTS.md`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/.agents/AGENTS.md).

* **Goal**: Elevate the CV visual presentation to a 100/100 recruiter standard, prevent arbitrary/non-matching PDF resume text scrambling, eliminate multi-column text interleaving, filter multi-page pagination noise and candidate header repetitions, support non-standard sections (Publications, Awards, Volunteering, Patents, Languages, References), guarantee bi-directional canvas-sidebar sync with directEdit fallback resilience, and certify via quantitative 30-area test suite.

  - **Spatial Column-Aware PDF Anti-Scramble Pipeline (`resumeParserEngine.js`)**: Implemented `extractPageTextWithColumns(rawItems)` calculating 2D bounding boxes and column gutters ($X \approx 20\% - 65\%$), resolving two-column text interleaving where sidebar headings (`SKILLS`) merged with main body headings (`WORK EXPERIENCE`). Wired into PDF.js loops in `StartModePage.jsx` and `JsonBackupModal.jsx`.
  - **Multi-Page Pagination Noise & Continuation Merger**: Added `PAGINATION_REGEX` filtering "Page X of Y" and candidate name repetition across pages; normalized continuation suffixes (`(Continued)`, `(cont.)`, `(cont'd)`) to merge cleanly into parent section arrays.
  - **Non-Standard Sections & Structured References**: Added `CUSTOM_SECTION_PRESETS` for canonical recognition of `publications`, `awards`, `volunteering`, `patents`, `leadership`, `affiliations`, `interests`, `additional`; built `detectGenericSectionHeader` with topical keyword guard; built `parseReferencesLines` and `parseLanguagesLines`; normalized uppercase titles via `toTitleCase`.
  - **Bi-Directional Synchronizer & Reducer State Integration**: `ACTION_TYPES.IMPORT_DATA` auto-registers all imported sections and references into `theme.sectionOrder`; `ACTION_TYPES.SET_SECTION_TITLE` supports both `key` and `sectionKey` guaranteeing reactive sync from sidebar inputs even when `directEdit` is false.
  - **Testing & Previews**: Executed 67-assertion two-way sync test suite across 30 areas (100% pass); executed 52-assertion master test suite across 24 areas (100% pass); generated visual previews (`executive_cv_preview_1789615987209.jpg`, `studio_sync_preview_1789616004002.jpg`); verified clean production build (`npm run build` exit code 0).

### B. Workspace Footer Cleanup & AI Pro Feature Gate Fix (TSK-234)
- **Problem**: In-session logged-in users visiting tool pages like `/ai-translator` were confused by seeing a massive, full-screen viewport `h-screen` landing-page marketing footer with three.js particles that asked them to "Start Free Now" (as if their session was ignored). In addition, because `ToolPageLayout` did not correctly destructure and enforce the `minTier` override prop, free logged-in accounts bypassed the Pro gate, loading empty layouts or desktop download funnels directly instead of showing the correct premium subscription block. Lastly, when attempting to push code modifications, git commits were bloated with massive tauri build binaries (`src-tauri/target/`, `src-tauri/*.exe`) exceeding GitHub's 100MB file limit.
- **Solution**:
  1. Updated [`Footer.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/Footer.jsx) to conditionally render as a clean, compact single-row footer on all non-landing routes. Added session checking to dynamically change the home page CTA button to "Go to Workspace" when the user is logged in.

  3. Configures git exclusions in [`.gitignore`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/.gitignore) to ignore all target compiled binaries, Tauri wix-bin, and PyInstaller executable packages. This reduced commit sizes from 3,374 files down to 327 files, allowing quick push success.

### B. Localhost Dev Server Resource Optimization (TSK-233)
- **Problem**: When running `npm run dev` or starting Vite on localhost, the Node.js process would spike CPU usage (18%+) and consume 650MB+ of memory. This was caused by Node's file system watcher recursively scanning the entire root directory, including the massive Python virtual environment (`.venv`), binary models (`public/models`), and dependency trees (`node_modules`), tracking thousands of files.
- **Solution**:
  1. Configured custom file watch exclusions in [`vite.config.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/vite.config.js) under `server.watch.ignored`.
  2. Blocked Vite from scanning or hot-watching the heavy runtime folders: `node_modules`, `.venv`, `public/models`, `dist`, and `.git`.
  3. This drops Node.js's idle CPU overhead to near 0% and saves hundreds of megabytes of RAM during local development.

### B. OCR PDF Universal Unicode Font Embedding (TSK-232)
- **Problem**: OCR on non-Latin documents (Cyrillic, Arabic, Chinese) resulted in blank square boxes ("tofu") in PDF viewers because the default Helvetica font inside `jsPDF` only supports standard WinAnsi characters.
- **Solution**:
  1. Downloaded the universal GNU Unifont (`unifont.ttf`) to `public/fonts/unifont.ttf`.
  2. Patched both [`OcrPdf.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/OcrPdf.jsx) and [`ocrPdf.js`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/pdf/ocrPdf.js) to dynamically fetch this TTF file during compilation.
  3. Encoded the font as Base64 in the main browser thread, registered it in `jsPDF`'s virtual file system (`addFileToVFS`), and mapped it as a custom font.
  4. Configured the invisible OCR text rendering layers to use `unifont` instead of `helvetica`, allowing PDF search indexing and text selection to work flawlessly for multiple language scripts without character corruption.

### B. Heavy Web-based Translation Engine Deactivation & Desktop Funnel (TSK-232)
- **Problem**: Running the 1.3 GB WASM M2M100 translation engine on standard client browsers caused severe load latencies (700MB download on cold boot) and high RAM consumption, leading to user frustration.
- **Solution**:
  1. Completely removed the client-side Web Worker inference logic and translation pipeline initialization from [`TranslatePdf.jsx`](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/document/TranslatePdf.jsx).
  2. Replaced the translation workspace on the web app with a premium marketing and onboarding layout routing users to download the DocForge Desktop App for local, hardware-accelerated AI translation features.
  3. This ensures the web app remains lightweight (~22MB max AI payload from the MiniLM classifier), responsive, and secure.

### C. Classifier Weights Compression & Build Optimizations (TSK-230)
- **Problem**: The client-side intent classifier weights file `classifier_weights.json` (11.38 MB) was extremely large and took long times to parse during initial worker warmup. Additionally, large backup model files were bloating the build directory and the Brotli compressor would crash with heap OOM errors when processing massive 300MB+ models.
- **Solution**:
  1. Applied threshold=4.0 coefficient pruning and remapped the active vocabulary from 10,666 to 8,407 features, shrinking `classifier_weights.json` by **85.12%** (from 11.38 MB to 1.69 MB) with **100.00% matching accuracy** maintained.
  2. Deleted four duplicate ONNX model backups (~36.5 MB) from `public/models/` to prevent directory build bloat.
  3. Optimized the post-build `compress_assets.js` script to auto-check file sizes: large files (>30MB) are compressed using standard quality (level 6) to avoid heap OOM allocation crashes, while all other assets use maximum Brotli level 11.
  4. Integrated proactive background model loading in `TranslatePdf.jsx` on page mount to load the translation worker and download the 700MB M2M100 ONNX models in the background while the user is selecting files, improving the first impression. Mapped a dynamic loading status badge and added unmount lifecycle cleanups to terminate the worker and fully free the 1.3 GB WASM memory heap when leaving the page.

### B. Un-OCR PDF (Flatten PDF) Offline Tool (TSK-229)
- **Problem**: Users need a secure, reliable way to make PDF files non-searchable and non-selectable (e.g. to prevent content copying, secure confidential text, or protect layouts from automated bots).
- **Solution**:
  1. Created a client-side offline **Un-OCR PDF (Flatten PDF)** tool that converts each PDF page to a high-resolution, static raster image (JPEG) inside [pdf.worker.js](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/workers/pdf.worker.js), effectively destroying searchable text overlays, font dictionaries, form fields, and vector layers.
  2. Created the engine wrapper [unOcrPdf.js](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/pdf/unOcrPdf.js) to manage the background worker thread.
  3. Registered the tool metadata in [constants.js](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/utils/constants.js) and the canvas operator definition in [canvasBrain.js](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/utils/canvasBrain.js)'s `TOOL_REGISTRY`.
  4. Added execution routing inside [StitchCanvas.jsx](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/StitchCanvas.jsx) for both dragging and dropping canvas execution paths.
  5. Built a dedicated [UnOcrPdf.jsx](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/UnOcrPdf.jsx) tool page featuring resolution quality configurations (Standard, High, Ultra) and mapped it to the `/un-ocr-pdf` route in [App.jsx](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/App.jsx).

### B. Auto-Enhance Output Premium Feature (TSK-228)
- **Problem**: Document images scanned using local cameras, compiled into PDFs, or extracted from documents are often low-contrast, shadowed, or slightly yellowed/dark, which reduces professional appeal and degrades OCR text extraction accuracy.
- **Solution**:
  1. Added a global "Auto-Enhance Outputs" setting switch in the Workspace Settings sidebar in [Home.jsx](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/Home.jsx), backed by `localStorage` persistence and defaulting to `true` to ensure a premium feel out of the box.
  2. Enhanced [imageToPdf.js](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/pdf/imageToPdf.js) and [pdf.worker.js](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/workers/pdf.worker.js) (the background pdf compiler worker) to automatically intercept page images and pass them through a canvas-based professional document filter (`contrast(1.25) brightness(1.05) saturate(0.95)`) before embedding them in A4 output documents.
  3. Integrated contrast-enhancing canvas filters inside [ScanToPdf.jsx](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/pdf/ScanToPdf.jsx) during local WebRTC/upload scan compilations.
  4. Patched [ocrPdf.js](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/pdf/ocrPdf.js) to automatically enhance page contrast/brightness before submitting page blobs to local OCR workers, dramatically improving detection accuracy for faded scans.
  5. Expanded the contrast-enhancing scan filters into [pdfToImage.js](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/pdf/pdfToImage.js), [compressPdf.js](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/pdf/compressPdf.js), [compressImage.js](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/image/compressImage.js), [convertFormat.js](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/image/convertFormat.js), and [resizeImage.js](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/engines/image/resizeImage.js), covering all conversion and processing outputs.

### B. Canvas Connection Warning Header & Auto-Heal Fix (TSK-227)
- **Problem**: When a newly added operator card had 0 connected inputs, the validation correctly flagged a cardinality mismatch but the warning panel at the bottom-left of the canvas displayed the hardcoded header "Format Mismatch Detected". It also displayed the "Auto-Heal Connection" button which would do nothing when clicked because no conversion tool was suggested for missing connections.
- **Solution**:
  1. Updated the warning panel header in [StitchCanvas.jsx](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/StitchCanvas.jsx) to dynamically render the title based on the issue type: `'Format Mismatch Detected'` for format-mismatch, `'Dependency Cycle Detected'` for cycles, and `'Input Connection Required'` for cardinality/missing inputs.
  2. Conditionally rendered the "Auto-Heal Connection" button only when a `suggestedToolId` is available in the issue metadata, eliminating useless button displays.

### B. Service Worker Dev-Caching & Chunk Load Resolution (TSK-226)
- **Problem**: In development mode, the service worker cached `/` and `/index.html`, which caused Vite HMR and WS updates to point to stale files, yielding a blank screen or a white page crash. In production, updates could cause a race condition or `ChunkLoadError` if old hashed assets were deleted while active clients loaded them.
- **Solution**:
  1. Disabled dev-mode registration completely in [registerServiceWorker.js](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/registerServiceWorker.js) (returns early when `import.meta.env.PROD` is false).
  2. Implemented a `controllerchange` event listener in `registerServiceWorker.js` to perform a clean, single `window.location.reload()` when a new service worker gains control.
  3. Bumped the cache version in [sw.js](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/public/sw.js) to `docforge-pwa-cache-v2` to force eviction of old caching layers.
  4. Configured a **Network-First** strategy for `/` and `/index.html` in `sw.js` to ensure online users always get the latest entry points referencing valid hashed chunks, while keeping the cache as an offline fallback.
  5. Implemented self-healing in [ErrorBoundary.jsx](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/components/ErrorBoundary.jsx) and [App.jsx](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/App.jsx) to catch `ChunkLoadError` and reload the page automatically once using session storage markers, clearing it on a successful mount.

### B. PDF to Word XML Layout Corruption Fix (TSK-203)
- **Problem**: PDF-to-Word conversions involving complex layouts, merged cells, or non-standard Unicode characters would result in corrupted `.docx` outputs that Microsoft Word refused to open (triggering a content corruption dialog).
- **Solution**: Patched [pdf_to_word.py](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/backend/services/pdf_to_word.py) to automatically check if LibreOffice is installed on the host system. If LibreOffice exists (primary), it converts the PDF to DOCX using LibreOffice Writer's compliant PDF Import filter, yielding 100% valid OpenXML markup that opens in Word without any warnings. If not found (fallback), it automatically falls back to `pdf2docx` in `safe_mode=True` (lattice/stream table parsing disabled) to ensure the file is not corrupted. Removed all settings checkbox toggles from [PdfToWord.jsx](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/src/pages/document/PdfToWord.jsx) to keep the conversion flow direct and automatic.

### B. Resolving NLP Pipeline, Classifier Training, & WordNet Expansion Gaps (TSK-172, TSK-223)
- **Problem**: Natural language command classification had severe lexical gaps (e.g. synonyms like "safeguard", "abridge", "coalesce" not triggering protect, compress, and merge) and local training was overheating the developer's GPU.
- **Solution**: 
  1. **WordNet & spaCy Verb Expansion**: Integrated NLTK WordNet and spaCy POS tags filtering in [train_intent_classifier_gpu.py](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/scratch/train_intent_classifier_gpu.py). Automatically queries verb synsets (`pos='v'`), applies spaCy parts-of-speech filtering, conjugates tenses (gerunds, past actions, 3rd person singular), and generates WordNet definitions directly as training sentences.
  2. **Custom Disambiguation Blocklist**: Created a strict `WORDNET_BLOCKLIST` to prevent synonym cross-contamination (e.g. blocking "cut" in edit-pdf to preserve it for split-pdf, blocking "lock" in unlock-pdf, blocking generic compress/shrink verbs in image-compressor).
  3. **Local GPU Safety (Throttle)**: Reduced batch size to 256, added a 5ms inter-batch sleep, and forced `torch.cuda.empty_cache()` every epoch, reducing sustained RTX 2050 GPU temperature and load to a safe 65–70%.
  4. **Google Colab Training Script**: Created [train_intent_classifier_colab.py](file:///c:/Users/Tanvir%20Alam/Downloads/New%20folder%20upscaled/scratch/train_intent_classifier_colab.py) configured to run training on Colab T4 cloud GPUs at scale. Uses 20,000 sentences per class (820,000 sentences corpus), batch size of 2048, and automatically triggers download of the resulting weights. Tested on 68-case suite (100% accuracy).

### B. Phase 5 Gap-Filler Master Upgrades & Patches (Completed)
- **LFI URL Filtering**: Patched local file inclusion security vulnerability in Playwright converter backend.
- **Visual Cryptographic Stamps**: Map drag-drop visual stamp coordinates to PDF points on the backend using pyHanko's TextStampStyle, rendering click-verifiable cryptographic fields in Acrobat.
- **Modular Resume Builder**: Refactored monolithic template styling logic into separate `resumeTemplates.js` file, added dynamic custom sections tab editor, and integrated a 250ms debouncer to preview updates.
- **JSON Import/Export (Resume Builder)**: Enabled backup/restore of resume builder forms and themes using client-side JSON files.

### G. Premium Chunked Upload Pipeline (TSK-77)
- **Problem**: Uploading files of 10GB+ directly in a single HTTP request causes memory crashes on client browser tabs and hits request body size limits on the backend.
- **Solution**: Added `/api/upload/chunk` endpoint on the backend (restricted to `pro` users) that appends uploaded chunks to files on disk in append mode (`"ab"`), keeping RAM flat. Implemented `uploadFileSecure` on the frontend that automatically slices files > 25MB into 20MB chunks and streams them sequentially. Patched all 15 backend-facing page components to use this helper.

### H. Client-Side OCR Multilingual Ensemble & GPU Binding
- **Problem**: Client-side OCR worker was hardcoded to only run the English handwriting model (`rec_handwriting_web.onnx`) with a restricted 95-character dictionary lacking accented letters. `OcrClient` was also hardcoded to `'cpu'`, ignoring GPU boost preferences.
- **Solution**: Upgraded `ocr.worker.js` to run an ensemble pipeline loading both `rec_handwriting_web.onnx` and `rec_multilingual_web.onnx`. Parsed `/models/multilingual_dict.txt` (6163 chars) and resolved characters using the highest confidence score between the two models per line crop. Added a custom `document.createElement('canvas')` -> `OffscreenCanvas` mock in the worker to enable true WebGL GPU acceleration. Parallelized model execution with `Promise.all` and implemented live line-by-line progress updates from the worker to the UI. Enabled the client in `OcrPdf.jsx` to load with `'gpu'` when settings GPU boost is checked.

### I. Client-Side OCR Performance Optimization & Collision Fix
- **Problem**: CPU memory copy overhead of multi-megabyte image arrays and running the heavy multilingual model on every line segment sequentially caused high latency (~5 seconds per page). Running inferences concurrently caused ONNX graph texture reuse output collisions.
- **Solution**: 
  - Enabled HTML5 Transferable Objects to transfer array buffers instead of copying.
  - Implemented `cropResizeNormalizeToTensor` in `ocr.worker.js` to perform bilinear interpolation and channel normalization in a single pass.
  - Kept loop sequential to prevent GPU collisions, but added a **smart threshold fallback**: runs the fast handwriting model first, and only runs the heavy multilingual model if handwriting confidence falls below `0.82`, saving ~50% inference load.
  - Saved backup copies of original implementations at `scratch/ocr.worker.js.bak` and `scratch/ocrClient.js.bak` for easy recovery.

### J. Parallel Client/Server OCR Pipeline
- **Problem**: Sequential page processing in both client-side and server-side modes made OCR slow for multi-page documents.
- **Solution**: 
  - **Client-Side Worker Pool**: Spawns up to `4` parallel `OcrClient` instances. Pages are dynamically dispatched to available clients. Canvases are resized to `0x0` upon completion to free up GPU VRAM instantly.
  - **Server-Side Thread Pool**: Uses a Python `ThreadPoolExecutor` (max 4 concurrent workers) inside `ocr.py`. Opens thread-safe separate document file handles.
  - **Ordering Guarantee**: In both modes, page results are sorted into index-mapped arrays prior to stitching, preventing page scrambling.

### K. WASM SIMD Parallel OCR — Full Page Batch Inference
- **Problem**: Even with a 4-worker pool, each worker ran line-by-line inference sequentially due to WebGL not being re-entrant. This resulted in 30 sequential ONNX calls per page at ~30ms each = ~900ms per page.
- **Root Cause**: WebGL execution provider uses global GPU state — concurrent `.run()` calls cause output tensor collisions. WASM is fundamentally re-entrant and thread-safe.
- **Solution**: Switched `executionProviders` to `['wasm']` only, set `numThreads = navigator.hardwareConcurrency`. Rebuilt the worker into a 4-phase pipeline: (1) build all line tensors, (2) `Promise.all` on all HW inferences simultaneously, (3) `Promise.all` on only low-confidence multilingual fallback lines, (4) ensemble pick. WASM maps `Promise.all` directly to its internal SIMD thread pool — all 30 line inferences run at once.
- **Files**: `public/workers/ocr.worker.js`

Distinguishes pinch gestures (`e.ctrlKey === true`) to handle smooth, cursor-centric canvas zooming (blocking native browser page scaling via imperative non-passive listeners) from two-finger swipes (`e.ctrlKey === false`), which now translate directly to panning the dot-grid workspace (Figma/Miro style).

### M. Desktop Offline Auth Bypass and Auto-Login (TSK-191)
